Session invalidate and validated read-only cache delivery share a short lock, so no quote can be committed after local logout; the lock is never held across network I/O.
- Continued Smart Data read-only HTTP pacing: a server HTTP 429 previously raised an error but allowed immediate calls again, potentially increasing provider/server load. Device reader now sets a bounded local 30-second monotonic backoff on either HTTPError 429 or direct response.status=429. During backoff, no index or equity HTTP request opens; local bearer remains available (429 is NOT a revocation), last-good cache unchanged, and after expiry normal authorized GET can proceed. Explicit 401/403 still scrubs bearer independently. Existing app-owned refresh lane budget, Oracle shared rate limit and 09:10–15:45 IST session remain separate gates; this is only local circuit breaking, not Dhan provider rate enforcement.
- Added fake HTTPS, deterministic monotonic-clock regression covering 429 with and without HTTP exception, no credential/error disclosure, no duplicate calls before expiry, resumed normal index GET and no false logout. Source-only offline tests, not a real Dhan/Oracle 429 observation. CI for this commit pending. No APK, Oracle production, old V1 files or live orders.
- NEXT: exact CI verification, then refine 429 Retry-After header handling and app-owned Smart Data readiness without claiming live production completion.

## V2 ATOMIC LOGOUT VS READ-ONLY FEED COMMIT — 2026-11-19
- [CI #194](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/actions/runs/44467244518) VERIFIED SUCCESS 416/416, security findings 0, UI wiring 9/9 at 242e4619452654682ce1f6433e567bd0243500c5. The app reader now discards a response if invalidated during network I/O, preserving WAIT/last-good.
- Closed the remaining race between *post-response* `session_active` check and `deliver_readonly_response`: introduced a small per-reader lock around bearer read, invalidation, and final validation/cache delivery as an atomic critical section. Network request itself does NOT hold the lock, so logout can invalidate a slow in-flight request before acceptance. Concurrent logout is serialized with the brief cache-commit step: either data was accepted before logout, or none can arrive afterward.

## V2 CONTINUED: ATOMIC DEVICE SIGN-IN SLOT GENERATION — 2026-11-19
- Verified [CI #192](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/actions/runs/44466881378) SUCCESS 414/414, security 0, UI source wiring 9/9 at 96dcad3e52c09746f9780af025f8fc54f54a8657: direct read-only HTTPS reader rejects invalid 0–70 equity roster before any network request.
- Audited `DeviceReaderSlot.sign_in`: old logic called `clear()` then captured `_generation` under a second lock acquisition, permitting an intervening concurrent login/logout to change generation between those actions. That could let an older pending login adopt newer attempt's generation and attempt to attach a stale in-memory reader. Fix: reserve/increment generation and detach prior reader in ONE atomic lock section per sign-in, invalidate detached prior bearer, and only attach if its generation remains current. `active_reader()` now reads status under the same lock. The server still independently authorizes each request.
- Added deterministic interleaved two-login regression with Event-controlled synthetic valid P-256 login: newer login owns active slot; older late-arriving session is cancelled and never replaces it. No bearer text exposed, no Android-native Keystore plugin or live transport enabled, no Oracle production changes, APK or live broker orders.
- Exact new-source CI still needs to complete; NEXT validate logs, fix any failure, then audit logout versus pending HTTPS delivery and make physical device/session acceptance explicit. V1 remains untouched.

- Continued immediately after mobile contract mock per-symbol replay correction at 57ff776c (CI #159 initially pending). Equal/stale individual rows are ignored; malformed or future individual revision fails atomically. The legacy numeric input remains for prior offline fixtures, clearly labeled as lacking reliable per-symbol freshness evidence; it must not be treated as proven live freshness.
- Added cross-boundary tests: TCS 10 vs stale 9 while INFY advances 8->11, replayed/equal 10/11 conflicting prices ignored, invalid future revision rejects all, read payload accepted by actual Flet DeviceFeedInbox. This is SOURCE CONTRACT only: NO collector authentication, no Dhan access, no production Oracle modification and no APK/order changes.
- NEXT: CI result, then explicitly audit numeric legacy fallback versus actual security/provenance requirements before any deployment, and keep the app's HIST/WAIT labels. Avoid unnecessary per-commit duplicate CI jobs and do not rerun historic failures.

## V2-FT2-ISOLATED-INDEX-EQUITY-REVISION-INBOX-20261118 — app-only two-lane authenticated-feed contract preparation
- **Prior gate:** FT1 signed P256 Master+3 delegated cross-worker SHA `6cc7d6e48aa6cd394046f90a3ca2ed3fb0561289` exact CI #40 `44316203468` SUCCESS. Last branch commit `b3e6911ad3682bcdb527b4c57ed7a403f97fa26e` docs-only.
- **Source-grounded risk:** existing `DeviceQuoteView.apply` had one revision counter for BOTH separately fetched `/v2-pilot/quotes` and `/v2-pilot/indices`. Two endpoints can legitimately return different monotonically increasing revisions. If an index payload revision=200 arrives first, a valid newer EQUITY feed payload revision=1 would be rejected as old. This is a contract-integration mismatch; no live production symptom claimed.
- **Scoped Flet-only fix:** `pilot_v2/android_app/src/feed_inbox.py` wraps two existing validated `DeviceQuoteView` instances. Explicit caller-supplied `quotes` allows only selected equities, `indices` only the three separate indexes, with separate monotone clocks and shared WAIT/HIST presentation. App-owned selections never written to server. `main.py` consumes inbox instead of one mixed clock. No HTTP listener/client, no actual bearer/session, no synchronous polling, and no API/Oracle module changed.
- **New focused tests:** `pilot_v2/tests/test_ft2_separate_feed_lanes.py` checks out-of-order independently valid lanes, source-only filtering, partial empty retention, invalid source/foreign feed fail-closed, deletion privacy and no invented LIVE. Prior legacy single-snapshot tests remain unchanged. Full isolated exact-SHA GitHub CI **PENDING** at commit authoring.
- **Deferred for final acceptance:** Real authenticated HTTPS fetch/client dispatch, native master pairing, app lifecycle persistence and Flet/Android runtime need verification.
